Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in equilibrium theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 488 and the invariant I(A,B) = 88 mod 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to equilibrium theory, we decompose the problem into 488 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 88. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
